### 5b. VERBATIM guards patch (G3 — NOT applied, NEEDS follow-up review + rebase onto Wave 3's config.go)

**REVISED by G8 (2026-11-19 ~14:05 IST) — follow-up review FAIL blockers B1–B4 + nits addressed in the text below; every anchor below was re-verified against the tree by the reviser (see the per-anchor notes). DO NOT APPLY until this revision passes re-review. B1: explicit clone-before-write-lock / release-before-persist ordering per mutating call site (step 3). B2: `findGatewayKeyLocked` variant chosen over a Validate exemption (step 5b, justified there). B3: all six wrong anchors corrected (step 7, [B3] markers). B4: all omitted live readers/writers added with a disposition each (step 7 + step 8). Nits: `c == nil` guards, verbatim DefaultConfig/Load edits, verbatim helpers, RestoreFrom comment update, step 4 now covers the Providers deep copy.** G8b (2026-11-19 ~14:10 IST) corrected 4 anchors + 4 nits below ([G8b] markers), each re-read in code by the fixer; re-re-review follows. **REBASED 2026-11-19 ~19:18 IST, final re-verification ~19:40 IST ([RB] markers) — re-re-review FAIL ~09:21 UTC (see §9): Blocker 1 DangerResetHook live writer added (step 3a + step 7), danger revoke-all `revoked` fixed to `len(snapshot.GatewayKeys)` (step 3a), keystatus CREATE + import paths enumerated (steps 3a + 7); EVERY anchor re-verified cold against the tree by content (config.go drifted +15 lines during the first pass; main.go then churned ~+75 lines/min under push-prep, so main.go numbers are the ~19:40 IST snapshot with content strings to locate each site — re-verify again at apply time, by content, never by memory). Patch still NOT applied; a FRESH re-review is required on a settled tree before any application.**

```go
// 1. Fields — internal/config/config.go, beside routingMu/pricesMu (after GatewayKeys :71 / Providers :72):
// providersMu is the ONE mutex guarding Providers — the slice header + elements
// (ProviderConfig contains Keys []string). keysMu is the ONE mutex guarding
// GatewayKeys. Same rules as routingMu/pricesMu: *sync.RWMutex so Clone shares
// it; nil means "not initialised yet" (DefaultConfig and Load initialise;
// EnsureProvidersMu/EnsureKeysMu cover literal configs); lock methods nil-safe.
providersMu *sync.RWMutex `json:"-"`
keysMu      *sync.RWMutex `json:"-"`
```

```go
// 2. Methods — beside EnsureRoutingMu (:2567) / EnsurePricesMu (:2579) [RB], mirroring them exactly,
// INCLUDING the `c == nil` guard the first draft omitted (both precedents return early on nil receiver):
func (c *Config) EnsureProvidersMu() {
	if c == nil || c.providersMu != nil {
		return
	}
	c.providersMu = &sync.RWMutex{}
}
func (c *Config) EnsureKeysMu() {
	if c == nil || c.keysMu != nil {
		return
	}
	c.keysMu = &sync.RWMutex{}
}

// 2a. Lock helpers — nil-safe, same shape as RLockRouting (:2589) / LockPrices (:2640) [RB]:
func (c *Config) RLockProviders() {
	if c != nil && c.providersMu != nil {
		c.providersMu.RLock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) RUnlockProviders() {
	if c != nil && c.providersMu != nil {
		c.providersMu.RUnlock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) LockProviders() {
	if c != nil && c.providersMu != nil {
		c.providersMu.Lock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) UnlockProviders() {
	if c != nil && c.providersMu != nil {
		c.providersMu.Unlock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) RLockKeys() {
	if c != nil && c.keysMu != nil {
		c.keysMu.RLock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) RUnlockKeys() {
	if c != nil && c.keysMu != nil {
		c.keysMu.RUnlock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) LockKeys() {
	if c != nil && c.keysMu != nil {
		c.keysMu.Lock()
	}
}
func (c *Config) UnlockKeys() {
	if c != nil && c.keysMu != nil {
		c.keysMu.Unlock()
	}
}

// 2b. Snapshots — deep copies under the read lock. The Locked variants do the copy with NO
// locking (caller must hold the read lock); the public Snapshot methods take the read lock
// themselves. Clone (step 4) and Save (step 5) use ONLY the Locked variants inside a held
// lock — calling a locking Snapshot there would take the read lock a second time, which
// deadlocks when a writer is waiting. This is the providerPricesSnapshotLocked precedent
// (config.go:2661, comment :2653-2660, whose text states the rule verbatim) [RB].
func (c *Config) gatewayKeysSnapshotLocked() []GatewayKey {
	if c.GatewayKeys == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := make([]GatewayKey, len(c.GatewayKeys))
	for i, k := range c.GatewayKeys {
		if k.Scopes != nil {
			k.Scopes = append([]string(nil), k.Scopes...)
		}
		if k.Guardrails != nil {
			k.Guardrails = append([]string(nil), k.Guardrails...)
		}
		if k.ExpiresAt != nil {
			exp := *k.ExpiresAt
			k.ExpiresAt = &exp
		}
		if k.RateLimits != nil {
			k.RateLimits = append([]RateLimitRule(nil), k.RateLimits...)
		}
		if k.TokenLimits != nil {
			k.TokenLimits = append([]TokenLimit(nil), k.TokenLimits...)
			for j := range k.TokenLimits {
				if k.TokenLimits[j].Enabled != nil {
					en := *k.TokenLimits[j].Enabled
					k.TokenLimits[j].Enabled = &en
				}
			}
		}
		if k.AccessSchedule != nil {
			sch := *k.AccessSchedule
			if sch.Days != nil {
				sch.Days = append([]int(nil), sch.Days...)
			}
			k.AccessSchedule = &sch
		}
		out[i] = k
	}
	return out
}
func (c *Config) GatewayKeysSnapshot() []GatewayKey {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	c.RLockKeys()
	defer c.RUnlockKeys()
	return c.gatewayKeysSnapshotLocked()
}
func (c *Config) providersSnapshotLocked() []ProviderConfig {
	if c.Providers == nil {
		return nil
	}
	out := make([]ProviderConfig, len(c.Providers))
	for i, p := range c.Providers {
		if p.Models != nil {
			p.Models = append([]provider.Model(nil), p.Models...)
			for j := range p.Models {
				p.Models[j].FixedParams = p.Models[j].FixedParams.Clone()
			}
		}
		if p.Keys != nil {
			p.Keys = append([]provider.ProviderKey(nil), p.Keys...)
		}
		out[i] = p
	}
	return out
}
func (c *Config) ProvidersSnapshot() []ProviderConfig {
	if c == nil {
		return nil
	}
	c.RLockProviders()
	defer c.RUnlockProviders()
	return c.providersSnapshotLocked()
}
func (c *Config) GatewayKeysLen() int {
	if c == nil {
		return 0
	}
	c.RLockKeys()
	defer c.RUnlockKeys()
	return len(c.GatewayKeys)
}
func (c *Config) ProvidersLen() int {
	if c == nil {
		return 0
	}
	c.RLockProviders()
	defer c.RUnlockProviders()
	return len(c.Providers)
}
// NOTE (G8b) — no GatewayKeysLenLocked()/ProvidersLenLocked() variants, deliberately:
// no caller needs a length under a held keysMu/providersMu lock (every Len call site
// in steps 7-8 is a standalone read), so adding them would be dead code. If a held-lock
// caller ever needs one, add it in this shape — no locking, len of the live slice —
// per the snapshot Locked precedent above.
```

```go

// 3. Lock-order doc — extend the routingMu comment's total order:
//   dangerMu / keyMu -> keysMu / providersMu -> pricesMu -> routingMu
// (one direction, never reversed: danger/key paths that touch GatewayKeys take
//  keysMu too; Clone/Save/RestoreFrom take keysMu+providersMu BEFORE pricesMu.)
//  ComboStore.mu -> providersMu is a second nesting into the same order:
//  ComboStore.providerByID (combos_api.go:365, func :361) [RB] takes ProvidersSnapshot()
//  while callers hold ComboStore.mu — handleListCombos :1001 RLock (:1002) and
//  handleGetCombo :1054 RLock (:1056) via view :420, Create :804 Lock (:875, via
//  create :861) via validateChain :545 and view :420, handlePatchCombo :1075 Lock
//  (:1090) the same way [RB] — and ComboStore.mu already sits above routingMu by
//  the same rule (mirrorGroup comment, combos_api.go:606 [RB]: "the lock order is
//  always ComboStore.mu -> routingMu"). No cycle
//  exists today: no Providers writer takes ComboStore.mu (the writers are
//  configProviderStore.Save and the keystatus/danger paths, none of which touch
//  ComboStore.mu), and no path takes providersMu and then ComboStore.mu.
//
// 3a. B1 — THE ORDERING RULE (this is what made the first draft unsafe; every mutating
// call site below MUST follow it — no exceptions). Go's sync.RWMutex is NOT reentrant:
// a goroutine holding the keysMu/providersMu WRITE lock deadlocks if it calls anything
// that takes the same lock again — Clone() takes the keysMu+providersMu READ locks
// (step 4), configPersist() IS cfg.Save (config.go:3558) [RB], which takes the keysMu+
// providersMu READ locks across Validate+Marshal (step 5), and RestoreFrom() takes the
// keysMu+providersMu WRITE locks (step 6). So a handler that holds the write lock
// across Clone/configPersist/RestoreFrom deadlocks itself the moment a writer is
// waiting (a read lock held by the same goroutine blocks behind a queued writer).
// The rule, per the tree's existing pricesMu discipline — pricing_api.go:462-464
// (comment: "The write lock is released BEFORE configPersist below: Save takes the
// prices READ lock, so holding the write lock across the persist would deadlock";
// the quoted sentence is :462-464, inside the comment block :457-471 — [G8b: the
// old :462-471 citation pointed at the whole block, not the sentence]),
// :533 UnlockPrices() then :535 configPersist(); main.go routingPersist (:3656) [RB]:
// LockRouting in the func body, :3717 UnlockRouting, :3718 cfg.Save — is:
//   (i) CLONE-BEFORE-WRITE-LOCK: take the Clone snapshot BEFORE acquiring the
//       keysMu/providersMu write lock (Clone's own read locks are then uncontended
//       with any lock this goroutine holds — the handler holds at most dangerMu/keyMu,
//       which sit ABOVE keysMu/providersMu in the order, so no cycle);
//   (ii) RELEASE-BEFORE-PERSIST: release the keysMu/providersMu write lock BEFORE
//       calling configPersist()/Save; re-acquire it ONLY for a rollback RestoreFrom,
//       and NEVER hold it across RestoreFrom itself (RestoreFrom takes it internally).
//   The handler-level mutex (keyMu/dangerMu) STAYS held across the persist — that is
//   safe (Save never takes keyMu/dangerMu) and REQUIRED: it is what serialises key
//   writers against each other between apply and persist, exactly as pricingMu stays
//   held across the pricing persist while only the prices write lock is released.
//   Per-site sequences (current code line numbers verified 2026-11-19):
//   - keystatus.go update (func :540; Lock :628, Clone :641, persist :711, RestoreFrom :719) [RB]:
//     keyMu.Lock; idx := find via GatewayKeysSnapshot() (no keysMu held); snapshot :=
//     Clone(); LockKeys(); mutate &s.cfg.GatewayKeys[idx] in place; UnlockKeys();
//     configPersist(); on error: RestoreFrom(snapshot) [NO keysMu held across it];
//     keyMu.Unlock (defer). The in-place `key := &s.cfg.GatewayKeys[idx]` mutation
//     needs the keysMu WRITE lock — a read lock is NOT enough (first draft said so
//     and was right on this point). Post-persist `updated := *key` (keystatus.go:726) [RB]
//     is read AFTER UnlockKeys() with only keyMu held — safe: a read-read against
//     keysMu-RLock readers, and GatewayKeys writers are serialised by keyMu (danger
//     paths the only other writers — see the KNOWN LIMIT above).
//   - keystatus.go delete (func :781; Lock :802, Clone :815, persist :817, RestoreFrom :820) [RB]:
//     same shape: snapshot := Clone() before LockKeys(); LockKeys(); splice;

//   - keystatus.go rotate (func :869; Lock :889, Clone :948, persist :951, RestoreFrom :954) [RB]:
//     same shape; note Clone currently sits AFTER the new-key issue (:907) [RB] — move it

//     keyMu.Lock; config.IssueKey (CPU-only, mints the key, no lock); snapshot :=

//     duplicate-ID scan + append); configPersist() with NO keysMu held (AddGatewayKey
//     released it on return); on error RestoreFrom(snapshot) with no keysMu held.
//     Substance verified cold: create ALREADY follows rule (i)/(ii) — Clone is taken
//     before any keysMu write lock and no keysMu lock is held across the persist. The
//     patch wires nothing new here; it lists the path so the applier and a reviewer can

//   keyMu and dangerMu are DIFFERENT mutexes, so a keystatus mutation and a danger
```

## 8. Packaging gaps (2026-11-19 13:53 IST run — hourly weakness analysis, third pass; research-log entries 496–501)

- **PKG-1 (HEADLINE, entry 496) — Dockerfile's documented `docker run` first-run flow destroys the gateway key it just created.** `runInit` (`cmd/codvian-router/main.go:4295-4340`) saves the first gateway key into config.json at `~/.config/codvian-router/config.json` (`internal/config/config.go:1217-1223`); both Dockerfiles declare `VOLUME` only for the data dir (`/home/nonroot/.local/share/codvian-router`), so the documented `docker run --rm -v codvian-router-data:... init` deletes config.json on exit, and the documented follow-up `docker run -d --network host` mounts nothing — `serve` then `LoadOrCreate`s a fresh empty config (bootstrap PENDING) and the saved key is dead. **Fix (one line each):** add `VOLUME ["/home/nonroot/.config/codvian-router"]` to `Dockerfile` + `Dockerfile.release`, and correct the Dockerfile header example to mount both named volumes; or move gateway keys into the data dir so one volume persists all state. Verify by running the documented commands verbatim end-to-end.
- **PKG-2 (HEADLINE, entry 497) — No CI workflow at all.** `.github/workflows/` holds only `release.yml`; nothing runs `scripts/ci.sh` on push/PR — first automated test run is inside the release job, after publish (workflow header, items 307–308). **Fix:** add `.github/workflows/ci.yml` running `scripts/ci.sh` on push + PR, under the same exact-pin policy as release.yml.
- **PKG-3 (HEADLINE, entry 498) — No lifecycle subcommands.** No `status`/`stop`/`restart`/`logs`/`health`/`update`/`uninstall` in the `main.go:517-568` switch; OmniRoute ships all six (`bin/cli/commands/{status,stop,restart,logs,health,update}.mjs`); our tray supervisor (`internal/tray/supervisor.go:87-160`) has Start/Stop/Restart internally with no CLI surface. **Fix:** add `status`/`stop`/`restart`/`logs`/`health` subcommands (status reads a PID file the tray supervisor writes + `/healthz`) and an `update` subcommand doing the verified binary swap, replacing the `/admin/update` notImplementedAdmin stub (`internal/server/server.go:1236`).
- **PKG-4 (entry 499) — Install paths fail open when checksums.txt is unreachable; cached npm binary never re-verified.** `scripts/install.sh:178-182` and `bin/codvian-router.js` `verifyDownloadedArchive` install WITHOUT any verification when the manifest can't be downloaded (a bad signature refuses, a missing manifest installs silently); `ensureBinary` returns a cached binary without re-checking it. **Fix:** retry the manifest download (3 attempts, backoff) then fail closed; store the verified sha256 alongside the cached binary and re-check it on every run.
- **PKG-5 (minor, entry 500) — No Makefile; no nfpm/brew in GoReleaser.** No `make` entry point (`scripts/ci.sh` + 40+ scripts must be discovered by listing); `.goreleaser.yml` has no `nfpms`/`brews` sections (PARITY "Extra install surfaces" MISSING row has no concrete fix). **Fix:** thin Makefile (help/ci/build/test/docker → `scripts/ci.sh`) + nfpm deb/rpm and brews blocks in `.goreleaser.yml`.
- **PKG-6 (minor, entry 501) — Docker builder Go version drifts from the release toolchain.** Dockerfiles build with `golang:1.22-bookworm`; release.yml pins Go 1.27.4; `go.mod` says `go 1.22`. **Fix:** bump both Dockerfiles' builder to the same Go patch release.yml pins and assert the match in `scripts/ci.sh`.
- **Already tracked, not new (entry 502):** `--version`/`version` subcommand fixed in tree (G6 flip pending, GAP-TRACK §6); `.dockerignore` thorough; npm wrapper tar/zip handling fine; `Dockerfile.release` healthcheck duplication is documented in-file (fold into PKG-1's fix as a single shared source).

## 10. Routing-strategies gaps (2026-11-19 16:53 IST run — hourly weakness analysis, third pass; research-log entries 504–510)

Third pass over routing strategies — passes 1–2 (entries 316–328, 391–398) were OmniRoute-only; this run audited OUR code against all 21 of those OmniRoute weaknesses. Three HEADLINE gaps, one copied bug, one minor hardening item. OmniRoute still v3.8.54; no new release.

- **RT-1 (HEADLINE, entry 504) — No total time budget by default: one request can run 10 minutes.** `DeadlineBudgetMs` (`internal/config/config.go:1024`) has no default — validated only `>= 0` (`:1536-1537`), applied only if `> 0` (`cmd/codvian-router/main.go:2776-2777`); `withDeadline` returns the ctx unchanged when unset (`internal/router/router.go:1960-1964`). Per-target timeout defaults to 120 s (`internal/router/pertarget.go:31-33`), MaxAttempts defaults to 5 (`router.go:894-895`, clamped to 200 at `:901-902`). Worst case on defaults: 5 × 120 s = 600 s wall time, plus queue waits (TotalBudget 300 s caps waiting only). The deadline machinery exists and maps to a clean 504 — only the default is missing. **Fix:** default `routing.deadline_budget_ms` to 300000 in config defaults; verify the stream path (`router.go:4882`, `withDeadline` wraps the stream goroutine) scopes the deadline to attempt selection, not the response body.
- **RT-2 (HEADLINE, entry 505) — LKGP pins never expire and are never cleared.** `LKGPStore.Lookup` returns the pin regardless of age (`internal/strategy/affinity.go:117-123`); `UpdatedAt` is stamped but never read; no TTL exists anywhere. `Clear` (`affinity.go:125-130`) has ZERO production callers. Pins persist to disk via `Save`/`Load` (`:148-207`) with no age check on restore, so a stale pin survives restarts. Recorded on every successful response (`internal/server/telemetry.go:136`); the `lkgp` hook hoists it first unconditionally (`internal/strategy/wiring.go:545-556`). Scope: only when the operator selects the `lkgp` strategy (default is `balanced`; `auto` has no LKGP stickiness — `Latest()` has zero callers). **Fix:** pin TTL (e.g. 15 min) enforced in `Lookup`/`OrderByLKGP` + `Clear` on any failure class including timeouts and slow degradation + drop expired records on `Load`.
- **RT-3 (HEADLINE, entry 506) — Fusion/chaos/jev panel bypasses the strict per-request budget gate.** `budgetGate` runs per attempt on the sequential path (`internal/router/router.go:4198, 5511`) and for hedges (`:4244, :5582`), but `runComboStrategy` (`internal/router/combo.go:213` fusion/chaos-mode, `:240` jev) fans out up to 5 parallel calls via `comboPanelCaller` (`:133-145` → `e.callChat` directly, no `budgetGate`); `finishCombo` has no budget check. The server pre-send check prices ONE attempt at model level (`internal/router/requestbudget.go:8-14`) and cannot see the 5× multiplier — a strict-budget request with E < B < 5E passes pre-send and can spend up to 5× the budget. Judge is heuristic/local, so the multiplier is ≤5×, not 6×. **Fix:** in `runComboStrategy`, sum the priced prompt estimates of all panel members before fan-out and refuse (402) or trim the panel when the sum exceeds the strict budget.
- **RT-4 (entry 507) — 16-factor mode packs ship cacheAffinity + resetWindowAffinity weighted 0 in every table.** `DefaultWeights16` copies OmniRoute's zeros verbatim (`internal/scoring/scoring16.go:325-332`: CacheAffinity 0, ResetWindowAffinity 0, Reliability 0); every `ModePack` has cacheAffinity 0 and resetWindowAffinity 0 (`:374-418`). The 16-factor scorer IS live for the six mode-pack strategies (`sixteenHook`, `internal/strategy/wiring.go:344-376`, wired `main.go:2504-2505`). The cache-affinity INPUT is never plumbed either: `sixteenInputs` leaves `CachedPromptFraction` at -1 always (`wiring.go:301`) though measured warmth exists in-process (ledger `CachedTokenFraction` via the 1-minute warmth snapshot); `ResetInSeconds` IS plumbed from ledger roll-off. **Fix:** plumb measured warmth into `sixteenInputs` and give `cacheAffinity` and `resetWindowAffinity` real default weights (plus a nonzero default weight for `reliability`).
- **RT-5 (minor, entry 508) — HookFactory silently maps unknown strategy names to priority.** `internal/strategy/strategy.go:490-492, 540-542`: `Parse` fails → `StrategyPriority`, no log. Unreachable from any user-facing surface today (config load rejects unknown at `internal/config/config.go:1456-1457`; MCP tool rejects at `internal/server/mcp_http.go:1553-1556`; combos API + dashboard update path validate) — hardening only. **Fix:** log a warning on unknown strategy name in `HookFactory`.
- **Already fixed on our side, do NOT re-raise (entry 509):** p2c distinct draws + health-aware score (322); nadir fail-closed divergence (323, GAP-TRACK §2); response validation ON by default (328a); hedging single-gated, ON at 150 ms, WITH 256 KB body guard (328b); weighted zero-total → uniform draw, not priority (321a); cache affinity measured, not a hash (393); context-relay allowlist enforced (396). **N/A (no such feature):** keyword/length complexity classification (325); self-healing exclusion (326); eval-driven routing (328c); per-rung USD budgets (391); session stickiness (394); pre-screen (397); agentic-stall failover (398). **N/A as a routing gap:** live quota fetch (320 — we never fetch live quota; coarseness already P2/P3); affordability pre-check (395 — no per-connection token budget; per-key affordability via M5 ReserveSpend). **Stale PARITY rows for the coordinator (not new gaps):** P23 "MaxAttempts no upper clamp" — clamp to 200 committed d21d9d1 (M4), `router.go:901-902`; P25 "hedging no body-size guard" — 256 KB guard exists (`hedge.go:14-16, :44`); P9 "5 factors only" partly stale — 16-factor scorer IS wired for the six mode-pack strategies, `auto` pool path still 5-factor.

## 11. Providers/models catalog gaps (2026-11-19 17:53 IST run — hourly weakness analysis, third pass; research-log entries 511–520)

- **CT-1 (HEADLINE, entry 511) — Direct-path tool-bearing requests have no capability pre-check; a capability-mismatch 400 is terminal.** `provider.Model` carries no capability flags (`internal/provider/dashboard.go:124`); no param-stripping exists on the request path (grep `stripUnsupportedParams`/`UnsupportedParams` in `internal/router/` + `internal/provider/`: zero hits). Auto path is strict (`ModelHasCapability`, `internal/autorouter/autovariants.go:1224-1233`; code-variant filter drops undeclared models, `internal/autorouter/code.go:55-59`), but the direct path sends `tools:` verbatim and a 400 ends the chain (`internal/router/router.go:3692-3712`, "the chain still stops on it"). **Fix (one line each):** record per-model capabilities from the live probe / models.dev where available (never guessed from the model ID); pre-check tool-bearing requests on the direct path; treat a capability-mismatch 400 as a single failover to a confirmed-capable target.
- **CT-2 (HEADLINE, entry 512) — Removed-provider blocklist exists but is enforced nowhere.** `CheckRemovedBlocklist()` / `IsRemovedDomain()` (`internal/catalog/removed.go:113-168`, 5 entries: puter, theoldllm, gemini-business, suno, opencode-keyless-only) have zero production callers — only `removed_test.go:38`. `AddCatalogWithOptions` (`internal/provider/registry.go:651-726`), `addProvider` (`:487+`) and `resolveCatalogForLoad` (`:758-806`) never call them. A removed provider can be re-added today as a custom provider or via config file with an explicit base URL. **Fix:** call `CheckRemovedBlocklist` in `addProvider` (covers catalog + custom + CLI) and in `resolveCatalogForLoad`; reject a custom base URL whose host matches `IsRemovedDomain`.
- **CT-3 (entry 513) — Dead providers listed in the live catalog feed with free-tier flags.** blackbox (`internal/catalog/providers_reference.go:2007`, HasFreeTier true), ai21 (`:2160`, HasFreeTier true), bytez (`:2840`, HasFreeTier true), galadriel (`:2143`) — all `BaseURLs: nil`, no provider-level deprecated flag exists in the `Provider` struct (deprecation is model-level only, `internal/catalog/deprecated.go`). Mitigated: catalog-ID add refused (no base URL) and the feed serializes `has_base_url: false`; but `FreeTierProviders()` still counts three dead providers in the 165 total. **Fix:** add a provider-level `Deprecated`/`Retired` field (reason + date), exclude retired providers from `All()`/the catalog feed and from `FreeTierProviders()`.
- **CT-4 (entry 514) — Keyless no-auth providers copied without the ToS-avoid flag or any emulated-tool-calling marker.** All 10 no-auth entries carry `ToSGrey: false`, incl. duckduckgo-web (`:5275`), cloudflare-playground (`:5292`), devin-cli-agentic (`:5258`), uncloseai (`:5394`), aihorde (`:5411`); four carry HasFreeTier true. OmniRoute marks duckduckgo-web `toolCalling: "emulated"` + `browserBacked: true` (`src/shared/constants/providers/noauth.ts:24-44`) and its surface as web-session credentialled (`src/shared/providers/webSessionCredentials.ts:258`). No `ToolCalling` field exists in our `Provider` struct. Mitigated: all ten have nil base URLs (un-addable) and no browser-backed transport exists in our codebase. **Fix:** flag the five entry-403 providers ToSGrey (or a dedicated marker) and add a `ToolCalling` field (`supported` / `emulated` / `unknown`) so "emulated" never counts as tool support.
- **CT-5 (entry 515) — models.dev sync has no truncation guard; an empty fetch records as a successful sync.** `Syncer.Sync` (`internal/modelsync/modelsync.go:768-924`) has no size/shrink check; a 0-provider fetch parses cleanly, plans nothing, and sets `lastSuccess` + clears `lastError`. Bounded: entries are never wiped (fold drops only operator-priced / removed-provider pairs), but a partial fetch applies partial prices with no warning. Feeds are unsigned plain HTTPS GETs (no sha256/checksum/signature in `internal/modelsync/`). **Fix:** reject a fetched catalog shrinking providers/models beyond a threshold vs previous state (keep last good data, record as failed sync); fail outright on a 0-provider fetch; pin the feed when models.dev publishes a checksum/signature.
- **CT-6 (entry 516) — LiteLLM parse inherits the field-vs-prefix misattribution class; latent, fix before wiring.** `ParseLiteLLMCatalog` (`internal/modelsync/modelsync.go:209-276`) takes the provider from the `litellm_provider` field and the model from the key after `/` with no cross-check (`deepseek/deepseek-chat` + field `"qoder"` → provider `qoder`, model `deepseek-chat`); duplicate (provider, model) pairs resolve last-wins silently (sorted keys, no warning). Bounded: `matchProvider` (`:358-377`) is exact-match only, and the LiteLLM Syncer is NOT constructed anywhere (only models.dev in `cmd/codvian-router/main.go:1930-1937`). **Fix:** reject/log-and-skip entries whose field disagrees with the key prefix, and warn on duplicate pairs — before Wave 3 constructs the LiteLLM Syncer. **CLOSED 2026-11-19 ~19:38 IST (R8 review PASS):** mismatch → REJECT with warning + duplicate FIRST-WINS verified in `internal/modelsync/modelsync.go:253-363`, 4/4 CT-6 tests PASS, full modelsync package 25 PASS; LiteLLM Syncer still unwired (Wave 3's job, not CT-6's).
- **CT-7 (entry 517) — Stored model lists have no fetched-at timestamp and no scheduled refresh.** `provider.Model` (`internal/provider/provider.go:96-116`) has no timestamp; `RefreshModels` (`internal/provider/refresh.go:77-127`) runs only from manual `POST /dashboard/models/refresh` (`internal/server/models.go:243-290`); the only timestamp is the call's own `RefreshedAt` in the response. A list fetched at add time can serve routing indefinitely with no age shown. Mitigated: failed refresh keeps the old list; success drops delisted models; new models arrive with unknown window/prices. **Fix:** stamp `models_fetched_at` at add/refresh, surface the age in GET /v1/models + dashboard, and run a scheduled background refresh with a staleness badge.
- **CT-8 (minor, entry 518) — "614 providers" headline counts 243 entries that cannot be routed as-is.** README (`README.md:56-58`) + dashboard present 614 unqualified ("Add one by catalog ID with just your key — no base URL to look up"), but 234 entries have no base URL and only 371 are `Routable()` (measured this run); the count includes search 21, audio 12, cloud-agent 3, upstream-proxy 2, web-cookie 37. Mitigated: `GET /api/catalog` serializes an honest `has_base_url` flag; `Routable()`/`MeasureBaseURLCoverage()` exist. **Fix:** quote the routable count alongside the total everywhere the headline appears ("614 in the catalog, 371 routable as listed").
- **Already tracked / stale PARITY text for the main coordinator (not new gaps):** (a) Row 589 WRONG overlay — FIXED in tree by G7: `mergeVerified` OR-merges flags (`internal/catalog/catalog.go:507-556`), review PASS, test-pinned; flip to FULL. (b) Row 588 deprecated/retired — text "no provider-removal blocklist" is now wrong: blocklist exists (`internal/catalog/removed.go`, 5 entries) but unwired (CT-2); flip text to PARTIAL. (c) Row 582 HasFreeTier "surfaced nowhere" — STALE: `has_free_tier` is serialized in `GET /api/catalog` (`internal/server/providers_add.go`), count now 165; flip to FULL with CT-3 as the residual. (d) Row 583 free-tier pools MISSING — PARTLY STALE: `FreeTierPools()` + `FreeTierRanking()` exist (`internal/catalog/catalog.go:726-801`) but have zero production callers and carry no budgets by design; correct text is PARTIAL (code exists, unwired). (e) Row 579 prefix convention PARTIAL — STALE text: `ParseCustomPrefix`/`ApplyPrefix` exist (`internal/catalog/custom.go:110-168`) but have zero callers and `ToProvider()` never applies a prefix; behaviour still missing, keep PARTIAL with corrected text. (f) Rows confirmed still accurate: 570 (614 total), 571 (380 with base URLs / 234 cannot be added), 574 (static model lists on 0 entries, measured), 576 (models.dev sync OFF by default), 978 (no LiteLLM sync, no hardcoded defaults). **CORRECTION 2026-11-19 ~19:47 IST (R7 + CT-8, R12 re-verifying): (f) is now STALE — post-CT-3 the total is 610 (614 − 4 retired), routable 370 (was 371, only ai21 routable pre-fix), FreeTier 162 (was 165); WithBaseURL 379 / TemplateOnly 9 / non-routable 240. Row 570's "614 total" and the 571 base-URL split need re-measurement text; the CT-8 headline fix (README :52,56,58 + README.v2.md) already quotes 610/370.**
- **Checked-and-closed, do NOT re-raise (entry 519):** 330 default-executor fallback — FIXED (unknown catalog ID is a hard error; no executor map); 331 four-lists drift — N/A (one catalog feeds registry, add path and feed); 332 unpriced=$0 — FIXED (pointer prices, neutral-0.5 scorer, UnpricedFailClosed default); 333 LiteLLM dead keys — FIXED in mechanism (exact-match only; latent misattribution is CT-6); 334 "keyless" label — FIXED in mechanism (per-endpoint auth resolution + key test; flag half is CT-4); 335 discovery no-expiry — PARTIAL (CT-7 is the remainder); 336 unsigned full replace — PARTIAL, no wipe possible (CT-5 is the remainder); 337 case-insensitive collisions — FIXED (exact keys, no lowercase index); 400 capability guess — FIXED on auto path, open on direct path (CT-1); 401 context guesses — FIXED (no provider defaults / substring heuristics; unknown window excluded from filters; no MAX advertising); 402 deprecated display-only — PARTIAL (CT-2 + CT-3 are the remainders); 403 emulated tool calling in auto pool — N/A in transport terms (no such transport exists), catalog-data half is CT-4; 404 authoritative discovery — N/A in that shape (no static list to hide), staleness half is CT-7.

## 12. M9.10-GAP-WT — Unpinned wrong-type/empty MCP error variants (P1-6+P2-8 review nit 1; queued, NOT M9.10-gate-blocking)

- **Status (2026-11-19 ~18:13 IST, gap-track builder, docs only — no code, no fixtures, no tests changed): QUEUED, severity LOW.** Behaviour is correct on every variant below: each refuses with -32602 and the expected message; only the pin is missing, so a future refactor could silently change a refusal message (or drop a check) with no test going red. The M9.10 gate explicitly blessed the fixture coverage as-is — the P1-6 comment in `internal/apitest/mcp_fixture_test.go:623-627` states the bar ("success + primary error branch per tool is the bar — wrong-type/empty variants of the same validators are not pinned") — and queued this as a tracked gap for a later slice. This entry makes the gap concrete.
- **Update (2026-11-19 ~19:10 IST, WT-01…WT-08 pin slice): WT-01…WT-08 PINNED** — 8 stdio fixtures in `tests/fixtures/mcp/` + record specs in `internal/apitest/mcp_fixture_test.go`, each asserting the exact -32602 message; no handler behaviour changed (no bug found). WT-09…WT-32 still QUEUED. §12 total now 8 pinned / 24 queued.
- **Update (2026-11-19 ~19:52 IST, WT-09…WT-16 pin slice): WT-09…WT-16 PINNED** — 8 stdio fixtures in `tests/fixtures/mcp/` + record specs in `internal/apitest/mcp_fixture_test.go` (ids 83–90), each asserting the exact -32602 message; no handler behaviour changed (no bug found). Note: the WT-09/WT-10 record specs (ids 83–84) pre-existed in the tree from the RT-5 WT-01–WT-10 filler; this slice verified them against the live server via TestRecordMCPFixtures and recorded their fixtures fresh (the 8 fixture files named in the RT-5 note were absent from the tree at slice start — re-recorded, not reused unverified). `go test ./internal/apitest/ -run 'TestMCPFixture' -count=1`: 4 top-level PASS, 90 subtests PASS, 0 FAIL (incl. all 8 new subtests). WT-17…WT-32 still QUEUED. §12 total now 16 pinned / 16 queued.
- **Counting rule (read before disputing the number):** one variant = one (tool, argument, distinct refusal message). That is the unit the fix slice works in (one fixture/test case per variant). By that unit the exact count is **32 variants: 25 wrong-type + 7 empty**. Why the review said "~20": the same 32 collapse to **24 distinct messages** (e.g. `name: must be a string` is one message on three tools) and to **~21 distinct code branches** once the shared-helper call sites are grouped (`resolveCostKeyID` serves three tools from one `optStringArg` call at `internal/mcp/tools_cost.go:183`; the three calendar caps share one loop call at `internal/mcp/tools_cost.go:270`). All three numbers are true under their unit; 32 is the per-tool count, ~21 is the per-branch count the review estimated. Scope is shape/presence checks only (wrong JSON type, empty/whitespace string, empty array/element); value-domain checks (unknown names, negative numbers, out-of-range, non-integer floats) are NOT in the 32 — the three unpinned ones of that adjacent kind are listed separately as A1–A3 below.
- **Method:** every validation call site in `internal/mcp/tools_routing.go` (helpers `:230-324`, handlers `:480-943`), `internal/mcp/tools_cost.go` (`:182-292`), `internal/mcp/tools_ops.go` (`:99-110`, `:219-249`), `internal/mcp/tools_search.go` (`:227-250`) was read; each branch was checked against all three fixture files (`internal/apitest/mcp_fixture_test.go`, `mcp_http_fixture_test.go`, `mcp_sse_fixture_test.go`) and all `internal/mcp/*_test.go` unit tests — by exact expected-message grep AND by wrong-type-literal grep (a code-only `-32602` assertion would still count as a pin). Tools with no arguments have no branches and are not listed: `router_status`, `provider_list`, `model_list`, `list_combos`, `cache_stats`, `cache_flush`, `db_health`, `db_stats`, `memory_clear`, `heartbeat` (heartbeat takes no arguments — `internal/mcp/tools_search.go:251`).

NOT counted: `optFloatArg`'s `must be a finite number` branch (`tools_routing.go:292-295`, NaN/Inf) — unreachable over JSON-RPC, since JSON has no NaN/Inf literal and Go's decoder rejects them before the handler runs; only a future transport passing decoded floats could reach it, so no fixture can pin it today.
- **Suggested fix (for the later slice, not this entry):** one fixture or test case per variant (WT-01…WT-32), cheapest as a table-driven wrong-type test per tool in `internal/mcp/tools_*_test.go` (drive `callToolErrMsg`, assert the exact message, not just -32602 — the code-only assertions are exactly how the create_combo empty-name/non-array branches stayed message-unpinned) plus stdio fixtures for the primary ones (wrong-type `name`/`model`/`task`, empty `task`, wrong-type `key_id` on cost_report + set_budget_guard, wrong-type calendar caps) in `internal/apitest/mcp_fixture_test.go`, following the P1-6 fixture naming (`error-<tool>-<variant>`). Do NOT touch the shared helpers to "fix" this — the helpers are correct; the gap is pins only. Rename consideration for the fix slice: `error-best-combo-empty-task` should be renamed or supplemented, since it currently pins missing-task, not empty-task (WT-18).
- **Honest limits:** (1) Enumeration is static (code read + grep over fixtures/tests), not a live-server run — no variant was executed over stdio/HTTP/SSE in this slice; a live run could only confirm what the code already says (each branch returns its message before any Source call), so the risk of a miscounted branch is low but the count was not runtime-proven. (2) "Pinned" means a fixture or test asserts the exact message or exercises the exact input shape; code-only `-32602` assertions were counted as pins where they exist (create_combo empty-name/non-array/empty-members, set_budget_guard limit-wrong-type, cost_report days range/non-integer) — if the coordinator wants message-level pins everywhere, those four are message-unpinned too and the message-level total would be 36, not 32. (3) Line numbers are the working tree at ~18:13 IST 2026-11-19; sibling tracks edit these files constantly, so the fix slice must re-verify anchors before writing fixtures.

  - **REVIEW G20 — FAIL (scoped)** (reviewer 1ed2efef, ran all tests cold, 2026-11-19 ~18:30 IST). The relay + Gemini code itself is correct and all tests pass. The FAIL is scoped: builder claim 6 ("model axis now enforced on every /v1 spend path that names a model") is false — POST /v1/batches executes arbitrary per-line models with no policy gate at all — so the Area 4 row 14 flip to FULL must not land. Do NOT send relay/Gemini back to the builder: that code is verified correct.
    - Relay gate CONFIRMED: `relay.go:398` calls `checkKeyTrafficModel(w, r, 0, head.Model)`; `head.Model` parsed from the body and validated non-empty at `relay.go:326-329` (`"model is required"` 400), well before the gate; gate sits before the verbatim/translate fork (~`:530`), before `throttleKeyDelay`/`tryEnterKeySession`, and before any provider call; `checkKeyPolicy` runs before `tryAdmit` (`keytraffic.go:360` vs `:365`), so a refusal consumes no slot; 403 shape identical to chat (`key_forced_model` / `key_model_not_allowed`, `admin_keys_api.go:290-303`); token axis still 0.
    - Gemini gate CONFIRMED: `gemini_native.go:1122` calls `checkKeyTrafficModel(w, r, 0, model)`; model is the URL path model (`handleGeminiModelAction`, `:1031-1047`, strips `models/` prefix, splits on last `:`); `geminiGenerate` rejects empty model at `:1082`, before the gate at `:1122`, before the body is read (`:1140+`) and before `engine.Chat` (`:1172`, stream `:1262`); both actions (`:generateContent`, `:streamGenerateContent`) funnel through `geminiGenerate`, so no non-model action escapes the gate; token axis still 0.
    - Tests reviewer ran: `go test -c` exit 0; `TestG20*` **5/5 PASS** (allowlist tests prove 403 + 0 upstream/engine calls on refusal, 200 on allowed model, RPM=1 slot rule). Regressions: set 1 12/12 PASS; set 2 20/20 PASS (18× `TestM813*`, 2× `TestMessagesVerbatimRelay*`, 0× `TestGemini*` — no such tests exist); set 3 20/20 PASS (`TestKeyTraffic*` + `TestMeStatus*`); extra `TestKeyDevicesRecorded` also PASS (absent from the builder's claimed 12 because its name has no `Enforcement` suffix). `go vet ./internal/server/` exit 0; `gofmt -l` clean on relay.go, gemini_native.go, g20_model_policy_test.go, keytraffic.go.
    - keytraffic.go dependency CONFIRMED: G20 did not edit `keytraffic.go` (its diff hunks are G15's, marked "G15 Area 4"); `checkKeyTrafficModel` exists at `keytraffic.go:351`; `checkKeyTrafficRequestsOnly` (`:416-418`) now has zero callers in non-test code (grep over `internal/` finds only its definition plus comments); if the G15 diff were reverted, `relay.go:398` and `gemini_native.go:1122` would fail to compile. G20 cannot land alone — land together with the G15 keytraffic diff.
    - A2A verdict CONFIRMED TRUE: `a2a.go:762` calls `checkKeyTrafficForChat(rec, r, chatReq)`; `chatReq.Model` set from the admit-time model at `:594`, from `a2aExtractMessages` (`:392-411`: `params.model`, falling back to `metadata.model`); `TestG20A2AModelPolicyAlreadyEnforced` passes.
    - Empty-model caveat CONFIRMED TRUE for A2A, but see Nit 1: `checkKeyPolicy` skips the model axis when `model == ""` (`admin_keys_api.go:288`), `GatewayKey.ModelAllowed("")` returns true (`gatewaykey.go:472-474`); chat, relay, Gemini all require a non-empty model before their gates, so only A2A allows a model-less call that escapes pinning.
    - Other spend paths verified gated, except batches (Blocker): chat (`server.go:4483`), legacy completions (`legacy_completions.go:406`), responses (`responses.go:1181`), embeddings (`embeddings.go:296`), audio (`audio.go:371`), images (`images.go:332`), media/OCR/video/music (`media_verify.go:781`) all route through `checkKeyTrafficForChat`/`checkKeyTrafficModel` with the caller-named model, each requiring a non-empty model before the gate. Provider-scoped chat (`provider_v1.go:292-330`) delegates to `handleChatCompletions`, inheriting the gate. Moderations (`moderations.go:214`) is local-only (no provider spend, model only echoed) and count-tokens is local counting — correctly out of scope.
    - Bare reads CONFIRMED TRUE, not built: GET /v1/models registered at `server.go:1491`, guard row `guard.go:225`; `VerifyKey` (`server.go:186-200`) sees only the raw key, so a VerifyKey hook could enforce IP/endpoint but not a model axis on a model-less list read. Correctly left to Wave 3.
    - **Blocker B1 — POST /v1/batches bypasses the model axis entirely.** `handleBatchesCreate` (`batches_api.go:159-243`) never reads the input file's models and never calls `checkKeyPolicy`/`checkKeyTrafficModel`; `runBatchWorker` (`batches.go:709`) executes each line via `engine.Chat` at `:812` with the line's model (`validateBatchLines` requires non-empty `body.model`, `batches.go:673-676`); grep over `batches.go`/`batches_api.go` finds no `Policy`/`ModelAllowed`/`ForcedModel` reference. The route needs only the `chat` scope (`guard.go:328`) — the same scope a normal chat key holds — so any restricted or forced-model key can spend on any model by uploading a batch file. This is an unenforced spend path claimed as enforced. Follow-up slice G21 spawned (policy-check each line's model at create time — the worker runs async with no `http.ResponseWriter`, so the check belongs in `handleBatchesCreate` after reading the input file — plus a test proving a restricted key's batch is refused).

## 13. M9.10-GAP-LEDGER — All-time per-key combo aggregate (GROUP BY gateway_key_id, provider) (KeyScoped ComboMetricsForKey review nit 5; queued, NOT M9.10-gate-blocking)

- **Status (2026-11-19 ~19:00 IST, nit-fix builder, docs only — no code, no tests changed): QUEUED, severity LOW.** `ComboMetricsForKey` (`internal/server/mcp_http.go:1568`) filters the 500 most recent ledger events (`RecentEvents`, `ledger.LogAll`) to one key's rows for one combo's member providers, because the ledger has NO per-key per-provider aggregate: `Summary()` gives `ByKey` (per-key totals across ALL providers — no provider split) and `ByProvider`/`ByModel` (ledger-wide — no key split), and `UsageSlices(key)` is per-key per-model but successful, non-shared rows ONLY (no failures, so no error count and no success rate). The sampling is honest and documented in the method comment (`mcp_http.go:1547-1550`): window `recent_500_events`, never `all_time` — usage older than the 500 most recent ledger-wide events is invisible, and a busy other key can push this key's rows out of the sample entirely, so a key with older combo history reads as no history (Requests 0), not a measured zero. This entry makes the follow-up concrete; it is NOT M9.10-gate-blocking — the sampled method is the reviewed, shipped behaviour and the gate passed with it.
- **Fix (for a later slice, ledger change, not a server change):** add a new ledger aggregate over `usage_events` — a `GROUP BY gateway_key_id, provider` summary (per-key per-provider requests/ok/errors/tokens/cost, mirroring `Summary()`'s `ByProvider` shape with a key split) — and have `ComboMetricsForKey` read it instead of filtering `RecentEvents(500)`, so the window becomes `all_time` like ledger-wide `ComboMetrics`. Keep the same counting rules (errors = ok=0, non-cancelled; cost = stored `cost_usd` summed unconditionally, including token-bearing failures) and the same unpriced detection (live price book), and update the method comment's HONEST LIMITS + the `Window` value when the aggregate lands. The `recent_500_events` sampling must stay until the aggregate exists — do not widen the `RecentEvents` window as a substitute (it is ledger-wide, so a busy other key still evicts this key's rows).

## 14. Auth/keys gaps (2026-11-19 18:53 IST run — hourly weakness analysis, third pass; research-log entries 521–525)

- **AK-1 (HEADLINE, entry 521) — No key groups: team-level model access control does not exist.** OmniRoute: `key_groups` + `group_model_permissions` + `key_group_members` (migration 065, `src/lib/db/apiKeyGroups.ts`), glob `*` allow/deny rules with optional provider pin, deny-wins, inactive groups pause grants but keep denies, wired into the request path (`apiKeys.ts:1822` `checkGroupAccess`), the `/v1/models` filter (`publishedModelLookup.ts:121-122`), SSO group resolution, and CRUD API routes (`src/app/api/keys/groups/...`). Ours: zero hits for `keygroup|key_group|KeyGroup` in `internal/` + `cmd/` — per-key `AllowedModels` + `ModelAccessMode` (`internal/config/gatewaykey.go:148-151`) is the only model scoping. **Fix:** add key groups (group + per-group model allow/deny glob rules + membership), enforced in `checkKeyPolicy` and in the `/v1/models` filter; a key whose groups are all deleted/deactivated/typo'd must FAIL CLOSED, never silently become unrestricted (OmniRoute's own fail-open trap — do not copy it).
- **AK-2 (minor, entry 522) — Fresh-install bootstrap unreachable over HTTP under Docker port publishing; `init` refuses once serve has run.** OmniRoute mints a random single-use token, prints it to stdout, accepts it for exactly one onboarding write, invalidates on consume (`src/lib/auth/bootstrapToken.ts`, #14296; owner decision: NEVER treat the docker0 gateway peer as loopback). Ours: POST `/admin/bootstrap` is TierLocalOnly by real TCP peer (`internal/server/guard.go:9-21`, "Strict loopback, no exceptions"); serve prints only `bootstrap: PENDING` (`cmd/codvian-router/main.go:4307-4308`) with no token. `init` works first (`docker-compose.yml` documents `docker compose run --rm codvian-router init`) but refuses once the config file exists (`main.go:4581+`), and serve creates/loads the config on first start — so serve-first under `-p` leaves no first-key path except deleting the config or curling from inside the container. **Fix:** mint a one-shot stdout bootstrap token on fresh install (random 24 bytes, printed once to the log, single-use, invalidated on consume, process-lifetime only) as an alternate proof for POST /admin/bootstrap when no key exists yet; never treat the docker0 gateway peer as loopback.
- **AK-3 (entry 523) — No coding-agent session aggregation; /v1/me/sessions lists only Responses chains.** OmniRoute `agent_sessions` (migration 229, `src/lib/db/agentSessions.ts`): one row per key + client session id, or per key + project inside a 30-min idle window; key-scoped fingerprint id (`as_<sha256(keyId + clientSessionId)>`); client/project/repo/path/source/git-branch, request/error counts, token splits, cost, unpriced count; upsert inside `saveRequestUsage`'s transaction so usage row and session counters move together. Ours: GET /v1/me/sessions is key-scoped and honest (`internal/server/mesessions.go`) and file-persisted (`internal/responses/responses.go:16-17`, `responses.json` — no restart-loss gap), but covers only Responses chains; chat/completions traffic from a coding agent has no session rollup, no client/project/branch attribution, no idle-window grouping. **Fix:** aggregate chat/completions usage into a persisted per-key session rollup keyed by client session id (or a 30-min idle window per key + project), recording client/project/branch, and surface it in GET /v1/me/sessions alongside the Responses chains; write the rollup in the same transaction as the usage row.
- **Already tracked / stale PARITY text for the main coordinator (not new gaps):** (a) Row 612 IP allowlist MISSING — STALE: IP allowlist/blocklist exist and are enforced via `ipListMatch` (`internal/server/admin_keys_api.go:133-145,179-180,210-223`; G15 nit 12: plain IPs compared as parsed IPs); flip to FULL. (b) Row 613 device tracking MISSING — STALE: device tracking exists (`internal/server/keydevices.go`, fingerprint = peer IP + User-Agent, surfaced in me/status `internal/server/keystatus.go`); flip to FULL. (c) Row 614 templates/import MISSING — STALE: key templates (`admin_keys_api.go:67-119`) and POST /admin/keys/import (`:308-439`, hash-only, idempotent retry, 409 on clash) exist; flip to FULL. (e) Row 611 per-key policy fields PARTIAL — text is stale in the other direction: IP allowlist/blocklist + device tracking + templates/import are now enforced, so the enforced set is larger than the row claims; rewrite the cell (remaining genuinely-unenforced fields, if any, to be named by the coordinator). Rules: 0-provider fetch fails outright ("0 providers"); 0-model fetch fails too ("0 models"); shrink beyond 20% of last-good counts fails (providers or models below 80% of last successful sync). Baseline is the last-good `Counts` (persisted in state file, restored by `LoadState`, survives restarts); guard applies only when previous count ≥ 5 (tiny catalog can't false-positive on a one-provider wobble); with no prior success only the empty check applies. Wired in `Sync` at `:961,987` (snapshot `prevCounts` under lock at start, guard right after `fetch`, before `Plan`); a refused catalog goes through the existing `fail()` path (lastError set, failure persisted, lastSuccess untouched, entries kept, `apply` never called). Threshold rationale in comment: a real models.dev edit removes a handful, not a fifth, in one fetch; a >20% drop is truncation/corruption until proven otherwise (error message says exactly what shrank). Unsigned-feed comment recorded (`:23-31` package doc, `:888-893` fetch doc): plain HTTPS GET, no checksum/signature/pinning; models.dev publishes none — no checksum invented. Tests: `modelsync_test.go` +229 lines, 4 new tests, full package **26/26 PASS** (22 pre-existing + 4 new: EmptyCatalogFails, ShrinkingCatalogKeepsLastGood, ModelShrinkKeepsLastGood, NormalFetchStillApplies incl. 10% shrink applies with no error + price change lands). `go vet` clean, `gofmt -l` clean.
